	const end = new Date(y.getFullYear(), y.getMonth(), y.getDate(), 23, 59, 59);
	const dayStr = y.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(y.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(y.getDate());
	const login = await new Promise(res => {
		try {
			my.getStorage({
				key: "loginInfo",
				success: r => res(r.data || r),
				fail: () => res(null)
			})
		} catch (e) {
			res(null)
		}
	});
	const LI = login || {};
	const token = LI.token || "m4_tAjNR3Sw7XpU5t_4DL5qKCNiP9stCRc__";
	const groupId = LI.groupId || "2575699441501575150";
	const thirdShopId = LI.thirdShopId || "2661337551157893458";
	const HEADERS = {
		client: "qianniu-agent",
		"Content-Type": "application/json",
		Cookie: "tanyu-agent-account=" + token + ";tanyu-group-id=" + groupId
	};
	function call(bizBody) {
		return new Promise(res => {
			my.sendMtop({
				api: "mtop.miniapp.cloud.application.request",
				v: "1.0",
				data: {
					path: "/api/context/agent-trace/paginate",
					headers: JSON.stringify(HEADERS),
					body: JSON.stringify(bizBody),
					method: "POST",
					options: JSON.stringify({
						timeout: 10000,
						cloudAppId: "56278",
						env: "online"
					}),
					sdkVersion: "1.5.9",
					protocols: JSON.stringify({
						"Content-Type": "application/json",
						"mc-timestamp": String(Date.now()),
						"mc-env": "online"
					})
				},
				method: "POST",
				needLogin: true,
				sessionOption: "AutoLoginAndManualLogin",
				success: r => res({
					ok: true,
					raw: r
				}),
				fail: e => res({
					ok: false,
					raw: e
				})
			})
		})
	}
	function getResults(raw) {
		try {
			const d = raw.data && raw.data.data;
			if (d && d.data && Array.isArray(d.data.results)) return {
				rows: d.data.results,
				total: parseInt(d.data.total) || 0
			}
		} catch (e) {}
		return {
			rows: [],
			total: 0
		}
	}
	const all = [];
	let total = -1,
		page = 1,
		empty = 0;
	while (page <= 500) {
		const r = await call({
			thirdShopId,
			pageIndex: page,
			pageSize: 100,
			beginTime: fmt(begin),
			endTime: fmt(end)
		});
		if (!r.ok) {
			console.error("请求失败", r.raw);
			break
		}
		const {
			rows,
			total: t
		} = getResults(r.raw);
		if (total < 0 && t > 0) total = t;
		if (rows.length) {
			all.push(...rows);
			empty = 0
		} else {
			empty++;
			if (empty >= 3) break
		}
		console.log("第" + page + "页 本页" + rows.length + " 累计" + all.length + " 共" + total);
		if (total > 0 && all.length >= total) break;
		if (rows.length < 100 && page > 1) break;
		page++;
		await sleep(300)
	}
	const sent = all.filter(x => x.sendType === 2 || x.sendType === 3);
	window.__daily = {
		all: all,
		sent: sent,
		day: dayStr
	};
	console.log("RESULT: 昨日" + dayStr + " 全量" + all.length + "条 筛选" + sent.length + "条 数据就绪")

### [AI · 2026-08-14 10:34:47]

拿到了，参数是 `noSendReasonList: [113]`（113=买家未开启自动接待）。顶部变量扩展成三个选项，完整脚本：

```js
(async () => {
	// ========== 参数配置（只改这里）==========
	const FILTER = 3;        // 筛选：1=已撤回, 2=侧边栏点击发送+编辑后发送, 3=未自动发送原因:买家未开启自动接待
	const DAYS_BACK = 1;     // 往前追几天：1=昨天, 7=近7天
	// ========== 工具函数 ==========
	const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
	const p2 = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
	const fmt = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(d.getDate()) + " " + p2(d.getHours()) + ":" + p2(d.getMinutes()) + ":" + p2(d.getSeconds());
	const now = new Date();
	const y = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - DAYS_BACK);
	const begin = new Date(y.getFullYear(), y.getMonth(), y.getDate(), 0, 0, 0);
	const end = new Date(y.getFullYear(), y.getMonth(), y.getDate(), 23, 59, 59);
	const dayStr = y.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(y.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(y.getDate());
	const login = await new Promise(res => {
		try {
			my.getStorage({
				key: "loginInfo",
				success: r => res(r.data || r),
				fail: () => res(null)
			})
		} catch (e) {
			res(null)
		}
	});
	const LI = login || {};
	const token = LI.token || "m4_tAjNR3Sw7XpU5t_4DL5qKCNiP9stCRc__";
	const groupId = LI.groupId || "2575699441501575150";
	const thirdShopId = LI.thirdShopId || "2661337551157893458";
	const HEADERS = {
		client: "qianniu-agent",
		"Content-Type": "application/json",
		Cookie: "tanyu-agent-account=" + token + ";tanyu-group-id=" + groupId
	};
	function call(bizBody) {
		return new Promise(res => {
			my.sendMtop({
				api: "mtop.miniapp.cloud.application.request",
				v: "1.0",
				data: {
					path: "/api/context/agent-trace/paginate",
					headers: JSON.stringify(HEADERS),
					body: JSON.stringify(bizBody),
					method: "POST",
					options: JSON.stringify({
						timeout: 10000,
						cloudAppId: "56278",
						env: "online"
					}),
					sdkVersion: "1.5.9",
					protocols: JSON.stringify({
						"Content-Type": "application/json",
						"mc-timestamp": String(Date.now()),
						"mc-env": "online"
					})
				},
				method: "POST",
				needLogin: true,
				sessionOption: "AutoLoginAndManualLogin",
				success: r => res({
					ok: true,
					raw: r
				}),
				fail: e => res({
					ok: false,
					raw: e
				})
			})
		})
	}
	function getResults(raw) {
		try {
			const d = raw.data && raw.data.data;
			if (d && d.data && Array.isArray(d.data.results)) return {
				rows: d.data.results,
				total: parseInt(d.data.total) || 0
			}
		} catch (e) {}
		return {
			rows: [],
			total: 0
		}
	}
	const all = [];
	let total = -1,
		page = 1,
		empty = 0;
	while (page <= 500) {
		const body = {
			thirdShopId,
			pageIndex: page,
			pageSize: 100,
			beginTime: fmt(begin),
			endTime: fmt(end)
		};
		if (FILTER === 1) body.ifWithdraw = 1;
		if (FILTER === 2) body.sendType = [2, 3];
		if (FILTER === 3) body.noSendReasonList = [113];
		const r = await call(body);
		if (!r.ok) {
			console.error("请求失败", r.raw);
			break
		}
		const {
			rows,
			total: t
		} = getResults(r.raw);
		if (total < 0 && t > 0) total = t;
		if (rows.length) {
			all.push(...rows);
			empty = 0
		} else {
			empty++;
			if (empty >= 3) break
		}
		console.log("第" + page + "页 本页" + rows.length + " 累计" + all.length + " 共" + total);
		if (total > 0 && all.length >= total) break;
		if (rows.length < 100 && page > 1) break;
		page++;
		await sleep(300)
	}
	const sent = all;
	window.__daily = {
		all: all,
		sent: sent,
		day: dayStr
	};
	console.log("RESULT: " + dayStr + " 往前" + DAYS_BACK + "天 全量" + all.length + "条 筛选" + sent.length + "条 数据就绪")
})()
```

顶部配置现在三个选项：

| FILTER | 筛选 |
|---|---|
| 1 | 已撤回（`ifWithdraw:1`） |
| 2 | 侧边栏点击发送+编辑后发送（`sendType:[2,3]`） |
| 3 | 未自动发送原因：买家未开启自动接待（`noSendReasonList:[113]`） |

执行完看 RESULT，再单独执行：

```js
copy(JSON.stringify(window.__daily.sent))
```

可以。把拉数逻辑包成函数，一次执行按三种筛选各拉一遍，挂成三个数组。完整脚本：

```js
(async () => {
	// ========== 参数配置（只改这里）==========
	const DAYS_BACK = 1;     // 往前追几天：1=昨天, 7=近7天
	// ========== 工具函数 ==========
	const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
	const p2 = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
	const fmt = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(d.getDate()) + " " + p2(d.getHours()) + ":" + p2(d.getMinutes()) + ":" + p2(d.getSeconds());
	const now = new Date();
	const y = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - DAYS_BACK);
	const begin = new Date(y.getFullYear(), y.getMonth(), y.getDate(), 0, 0, 0);
	const end = new Date(y.getFullYear(), y.getMonth(), y.getDate(), 23, 59, 59);
	const dayStr = y.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(y.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(y.getDate());
	const login = await new Promise(res => {
		try {
			my.getStorage({
				key: "loginInfo",
				success: r => res(r.data || r),
				fail: () => res(null)
			})
		} catch (e) {
			res(null)
		}
	});
	const LI = login || {};
	const token = LI.token || "m4_tAjNR3Sw7XpU5t_4DL5qKCNiP9stCRc__";
	const groupId = LI.groupId || "2575699441501575150";
	const thirdShopId = LI.thirdShopId || "2661337551157893458";
	const HEADERS = {
		client: "qianniu-agent",
		"Content-Type": "application/json",
		Cookie: "tanyu-agent-account=" + token + ";tanyu-group-id=" + groupId
	};
	function call(bizBody) {
		return new Promise(res => {
			my.sendMtop({
				api: "mtop.miniapp.cloud.application.request",
				v: "1.0",
				data: {
					path: "/api/context/agent-trace/paginate",
					headers: JSON.stringify(HEADERS),
					body: JSON.stringify(bizBody),
					method: "POST",
					options: JSON.stringify({
						timeout: 10000,
						cloudAppId: "56278",
						env: "online"
					}),
					sdkVersion: "1.5.9",
					protocols: JSON.stringify({
						"Content-Type": "application/json",
						"mc-timestamp": String(Date.now()),
						"mc-env": "online"
					})
				},
				method: "POST",
				needLogin: true,
				sessionOption: "AutoLoginAndManualLogin",
				success: r => res({
					ok: true,
					raw: r
				}),
				fail: e => res({
					ok: false,
					raw: e
				})
			})
		})
	}
	function getResults(raw) {
		try {
			const d = raw.data && raw.data.data;
			if (d && d.data && Array.isArray(d.data.results)) return {
				rows: d.data.results,
				total: parseInt(d.data.total) || 0
			}
		} catch (e) {}
		return {
			rows: [],
			total: 0
		}
	}
	// ========== 拉数函数（extra 为筛选参数，如 {ifWithdraw:1}）==========
	async function fetchAll(extra) {
		const all = [];
		let total = -1,
			page = 1,
			empty = 0;
		while (page <= 500) {
			const body = {
				thirdShopId,
				pageIndex: page,
				pageSize: 100,
				beginTime: fmt(begin),
				endTime: fmt(end),
				...extra
			};
			const r = await call(body);
			if (!r.ok) {
				console.error("请求失败", r.raw);
				break
			}
			const {
				rows,
				total: t
			} = getResults(r.raw);
			if (total < 0 && t > 0) total = t;
			if (rows.length) {
				all.push(...rows);
				empty = 0
			} else {
				empty++;
				if (empty >= 3) break
			}
			if (total > 0 && all.length >= total) break;
			if (rows.length < 100 && page > 1) break;
			page++;
			await sleep(300)
		}
		return all;
	}
	// ========== 三种筛选各拉一遍 ==========
	const withdraw = await fetchAll({ ifWithdraw: 1 });          // 已撤回
	const sent = await fetchAll({ sendType: [2, 3] });           // 侧边栏点击发送+编辑后发送
	const noSend = await fetchAll({ noSendReasonList: [113] });  // 未自动发送原因:买家未开启自动接待
	window.__daily = {
		withdraw: withdraw,
		sent: sent,
		noSend: noSend,
		day: dayStr
	};
	console.log("RESULT: " + dayStr + " 已撤回" + withdraw.length + "条 侧边栏+编辑后" + sent.length + "条 未自动发送" + noSend.length + "条 数据就绪")
})()
```

```js
						env: "online"
					}),
					sdkVersion: "1.5.9",
					protocols: JSON.stringify({
						"Content-Type": "application/json",
						"mc-timestamp": String(Date.now()),
						"mc-env": "online"
					})
				},
				method: "POST",
				needLogin: true,
				sessionOption: "AutoLoginAndManualLogin",
				success: r => res({
					ok: true,
					raw: r
				}),
				fail: e => res({
					ok: false,
					raw: e
				})
			})
		})
	}
	function getResults(raw) {
		try {
			const d = raw.data && raw.data.data;
			if (d && d.data && Array.isArray(d.data.results)) return {
				rows: d.data.results,
				total: parseInt(d.data.total) || 0
			}
		} catch (e) {}
		return {
			rows: [],
			total: 0
		}
	}
	// ========== 拉数函数（name=显示名, extra=筛选参数）==========
	async function fetchAll(name, extra) {
		console.log("==== 开始拉取：" + name + " ====");
		const all = [];
		let total = -1,
			page = 1,
			empty = 0;
		while (page <= 500) {
			const body = {
				thirdShopId,
				pageIndex: page,
				pageSize: 100,
				beginTime: fmt(begin),
				endTime: fmt(end),
				...extra
			};
			const r = await call(body);
			if (!r.ok) {
				console.error("[" + name + "] 请求失败", r.raw);
				break
			}
			const {
				rows,
				total: t
			} = getResults(r.raw);
			if (total < 0 && t > 0) total = t;
			if (rows.length) {
				all.push(...rows);
				empty = 0
			} else {
				empty++;
				if (empty >= 3) break
			}
			console.log("[" + name + "] 第" + page + "页 本页" + rows.length + " 累计" + all.length + " 共" + total);
			if (total > 0 && all.length >= total) break;
			if (rows.length < 100 && page > 1) break;
			page++;
			await sleep(300)
		}
		console.log("==== 完成：" + name + " " + all.length + "条 ====");
		return all;
	}
	// ========== 三种筛选各拉一遍 ==========
	const withdraw = await fetchAll("已撤回", { ifWithdraw: 1 });          // 已撤回
	const sent = await fetchAll("侧边栏+编辑后", { sendType: [2, 3] });     // 侧边栏点击发送+编辑后发送
	const noSend = await fetchAll("未自动发送", { noSendReasonList: [113] }); // 未自动发送原因:买家未开启自动接待
	// ========== 过滤成 sellerAccount 二维列表 ==========
	const sellerList = [withdraw, sent, noSend].map(a => a.map(x => x.sellerAccount));
	window.__daily = {
		withdraw: withdraw,
		sent: sent,
		noSend: noSend,
		day: dayStr
	};
	window.__sellerList = sellerList;
	console.log("RESULT: " + dayStr + " 已撤回" + withdraw.length + "条 侧边栏+编辑后" + sent.length + "条 未自动发送" + noSend.length + "条 数据就绪")
})()
```

回答你的两个疑问：

1. **三种数据确实都请求了**：脚本末尾 `fetchAll` 被调用三次，筛选参数分别是 `ifWithdraw:1`、`sendType:[2,3]`、`noSendReasonList:[113]`。现在每拉一组都有 `==== 开始拉取：已撤回 ====` 和每页进度，能看到三次独立过程。

2. **配置只有一个 1 是对的**：这版是"三种筛选**同时**拉取"，不需要 FILTER 切换——筛选方式已经写死在三次 `fetchAll` 调用里（那三行）。所以顶部只有 `DAYS_BACK` 一个参数，这就是这版的全部配置。

执行完看到三个 `==== 完成 ====` 和最终 RESULT，再执行：

```js
copy(JSON.stringify(window.__sellerList))
```

### [用户 · 2026-08-14 10:50:45]

等待和确认数据加载完成的时间有很大的不确定性，是否有方法能直接导出或者能直接让它更新到我的剪切板上呢

### [AI · 2026-08-14 10:51:14]

核心问题：千牛精简 DevTools 里 `copy` 只能独立命令用，脚本内调不了；`fetch` 上传被 CEF 拦截。但还有一个没测过的标准 Web API——`navigator.clipboard.writeText`，它不走 `copy` 命令，如果千牛 CEF 放行，脚本内就能直接写剪贴板，彻底消除"等待确认"环节。

**第 1 步，先测它是否可用**（strict-webview 执行）：

```js
(async()=>{ try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText('tanyu-clip-test-123'); console.log('OK'); } catch(e){ console.log('FAIL', e); } })()
```

然后打开记事本粘贴，看是否出现 `tanyu-clip-test-123`。

**如果可用**，脚本末尾直接写剪贴板，改成这样（在合并版脚本末尾 `console.log("RESULT...")` 之前插入）：

```js
try {
	await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(sellerList));
	console.log("已写入剪贴板");
} catch (e) {
	console.log("剪贴板写入失败", e);
}
```

影刀流程变成：粘贴脚本 → 轮询剪贴板直到内容变成 `[` 开头的 JSON → 直接读取，全程无人工确认、无固定等待。

**如果不可用**（`FAIL`），维持两段式，但加一个影刀可检测的完成标记：脚本末尾往 iframe DOM 插一个元素：

```js
const el = document.createElement("div");
el.id = "tanyu-done";
el.textContent = "DONE " + dayStr + " W" + withdraw.length + " S" + sent.length + " N" + noSend.length;
document.body.appendChild(el);
```

影刀在粘贴脚本后轮询检测 `#tanyu-done` 是否存在（出现即拉完），再自动粘贴 copy 命令读剪贴板——等待由"检测标记"替代"猜时间"。

先跑第 1 步测试，把 `OK` 或 `FAIL` 结果告诉我，再给你对应的完整脚本。

### [用户 · 2026-08-14 10:52:42]

### [AI · 2026-08-14 10:52:58]

拉数必须在 strict-webview 里做——`my` 只存在于这个跨域 iframe，top 层 `my is not defined`，数据拿不到。

但**写剪贴板不需要去 top**：`navigator.clipboard` 是浏览器标准 API，剪贴板是全局共享的，iframe 内调用 `writeText` 写入的就是系统剪贴板，和 top 层效果一样。所以直接在 strict-webview 里测那条命令即可，看 `OK` 还是 `FAIL`。

只有一种情况才需要 top 中转：iframe 内 `clipboard` 因跨域 Permissions-Policy 被禁（报 `NotAllowedError`）。那时可以用 postMessage 把数据抛给 top，top 侧再写剪贴板，但 top 页面脚本同样依赖 clipboard API 或 `copy`，而且还得先在 top console 挂一段监听器，等于变回两段式，不值得优先试。

先跑测试：

```js
(async()=>{ try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText('tanyu-clip-test-123'); console.log('OK'); } catch(e){ console.log('FAIL', e); } })()
```

粘贴到记事本验证后把结果告诉我。

### [AI · 2026-08-14 10:53:51]

Console 里显示的是 `OK` 还是 `FAIL`？这个很关键：

- 如果显示 `FAIL`：是被拦截了，需要看后面的错误类型（比如 `NotAllowedError`），告诉我错误内容。
- 如果显示 `OK` 但剪贴板没内容：可能写入成功了但读的是别处的剪贴板，或者被系统剪贴板历史覆盖。可以再试一次，这次执行后**立刻**在记事本里 Ctrl+V 看结果。

再补一个验证命令（直接读回剪贴板确认）：

```js
navigator.clipboard.readText().then(t=>console.log("READ:", t)).catch(e=>console.log("READ_FAIL:", e))
```

它会把当前剪贴板内容打出来，这样能判断到底是没写进去还是粘贴环节的问题。把输出发我。

### [AI · 2026-08-14 10:54:47]

`NotAllowedError: Document is not focused` 说明这条路关闭了：DevTools 打开时焦点在 DevTools 上，页面文档不聚焦，`clipboard` API 一律拒绝（`writeText` 也一样，你之前没看到内容就是这个原因）。脚本内直接写剪贴板在千牛这个场景无解。

回到两段式，但把"等待确认"变成**影刀自动重试**，不需要你盯 RESULT，也不依赖猜时间：

**影刀流程（全自动）**

1. 粘贴拉数脚本（上一版合并脚本，不用改）
2. 固定等待 60 秒（第一次拉数，三组数据同时拉，含翻页，一般够）
3. 进入循环（最多 5 次）：
   - 在 Console 粘贴并执行 `copy(JSON.stringify(window.__sellerList))`
   - 读取剪贴板内容
   - 校验：内容以 `[` 开头且 `JSON.parse` 成功、长度大于阈值
   - 校验通过 → 跳出循环，继续后续处理
   - 校验失败 → 等待 10 秒，重试第 3 步
4. 剪贴板拿到的就是二维列表 JSON

关键点：`window.__sellerList` 在拉数完成后一直存在，`copy` 重复执行没有副作用，所以"重试 copy 直到剪贴板有效"是安全的，等待时间长短无所谓——数据没拉完就再等一轮，拉完了第一次 copy 就成功。

拉数脚本保持上一版不变（带进度日志的合并版）。需要的话我可以把这段影刀流程补进你的执行文档。


- 如果有输出（返回 JSON 页面列表）：千牛开放了 CDP，影刀可以直接调 `Runtime.evaluate` 指定 iframe 的 contextId 执行脚本，全自动，连 Console 都不用开。
- 如果没输出：调试端口没开，CDP 这条路也走不通，维持两步式。

```js
window.__mTopLog=[];

my.sendMtop=function(o){
  try{ window.__mTopLog.push(JSON.stringify(o)); }catch(e){}
  return _orig(o);
};
console.log("hook已挂载");
```

然后操作页面：进入数据罗盘 → 打开"服务效率" → 再打开"AI性能"，回 Console 执行：

```js
window.__mTopLog.join('\n')
```

### [用户 · 2026-08-14 11:52:23]

500:::Maximum call stack size exceeded

### [AI · 2026-08-14 11:52:38]

多半是 hook 挂重复了——之前挂过一次，这次又包一层，`_orig` 链式递归导致爆栈。另外 `.bind(my)` 在小程序运行时上也可能触发代理递归。

先刷新页面（重新加载 iframe，把旧 hook 清掉），再执行一次，用不带 bind 的写法：

```js
window.__mTopLog=[];
const __origMtop = my.sendMtop;
my.sendMtop = function(o){

};
```

接口确认：一个 `metrics/query` 请求就返回全部数据，分 `service`（服务效率）和 `ai`（AI 性能）两组，按日期排列。直接出拉数脚本，在 strict-webview 执行：

```js
(async () => {
	// ========== 参数配置（只改这里）==========
	const DAYS_BACK = 1;     // 往前追几天：1=昨天, 7=近7天（接口按区间返回，改大自动返回多天）
	// ========== 工具函数 ==========
	const p2 = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
	const fmtDate = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(d.getDate());
	const now = new Date();
	const y = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - DAYS_BACK);
	const startTime = fmtDate(y);
	const endTime = fmtDate(y);
	const login = await new Promise(res => {
		try {
			my.getStorage({key:"loginInfo", success:r=>res(r.data||r), fail:()=>res(null)})
		} catch(e) { res(null) }
	});
	const LI = login || {};
	const token = LI.token || "m4_tAjNR3Sw7XpU5t_4DL5qKCNiP9stCRc__";
	const groupId = LI.groupId || "2575699441501575150";
	const thirdShopId = LI.thirdShopId || "2661337551157893458";
	const HEADERS = {client:"qianniu-agent","Content-Type":"application/json",Cookie:"tanyu-agent-account="+token+";tanyu-group-id="+groupId};
	const r = await new Promise(res => {
		my.sendMtop({
			api:"mtop.miniapp.cloud.application.request",
			v:"1.0",
			data:{
				path:"/api/data-service/compass/metrics/query",
				headers:JSON.stringify(HEADERS),
				body:JSON.stringify({thirdShopId,startTime,endTime}),
				method:"POST",
				options:JSON.stringify({timeout:10000,cloudAppId:"56278",env:"online"}),
				sdkVersion:"1.5.9",
				protocols:JSON.stringify({"Content-Type":"application/json","mc-timestamp":String(Date.now()),"mc-env":"online"})
			},
			method:"POST",
			needLogin:true,
			sessionOption:"AutoLoginAndManualLogin",
			success:r=>res({ok:true,raw:r}),
			fail:e=>res({ok:false,raw:e})
		})
	});
	if (!r.ok) {
		console.error("请求失败", r.raw);
		return;
	}
	let service = [], ai = [];
	try {
		const d = r.raw.data && r.raw.data.data;
		if (d && d.service && d.service.rows) service = d.service.rows;
		if (d && d.ai && d.ai.rows) ai = d.ai.rows;
	} catch(e) {}
	window.__compass = { service: service, ai: ai, day: startTime };
	console.log("RESULT: " + startTime + " 服务效率" + service.length + "天 AI性能" + ai.length + "天 数据就绪")
})()
```


```json
{
  "service": [
    {"date": "2026-08-13", "service_response_avg_sec": 19.29, "service_first_response_avg_sec": 7.01, "service_consult_cnt": 740, "service_3m_response_rate": 99.69, "service_consult_msg_cnt": 5063}
  ],
  "ai": [
    {"date": "2026-08-13", "ai_consult_response_rate": 66.84, "ai_consult_response_accept_cnt": 838, "ai_consult_response_accept_rate": 13.42, "ai_consult_response_risk_cnt": 1210, "ai_consult_response_risk_rate": 19.37, "ai_consult_response_flow_avg_sec": 6.43, "ai_consult_response_accept_auto_cnt": 814, "ai_consult_response_accept_manual_cnt": 24, "ai_agent_card_event_cnt": 6246, "ai_consult_msg_cnt": 7113}
  ],
  "day": "2026-08-13"
}
```

字段含义：
- **service（服务效率）**：`service_response_avg_sec`=平均响应秒数，`service_first_response_avg_sec`=平均首次响应秒数，`service_consult_cnt`=咨询人数，`service_3m_response_rate`=3 分钟响应率（%），`service_consult_msg_cnt`=咨询消息数
- **ai（AI 性能）**：`ai_consult_response_rate`=AI 响应率，`ai_consult_response_accept_cnt/accept_rate`=采纳数/采纳率，`ai_consult_response_risk_cnt/risk_rate`=风险数/风险率，`ai_consult_response_flow_avg_sec`=平均流转秒数，`ai_consult_response_accept_auto_cnt`=自动采纳数，`ai_consult_response_accept_manual_cnt`=人工采纳数，`ai_agent_card_event_cnt`=AI 卡片事件数，`ai_consult_msg_cnt`=AI 参与消息数

需要近 7 天就把 `DAYS_BACK` 改成 7，接口会返回 7 天的 rows。

### [AI · 2026-08-14 11:58:35]

可以，数据罗盘如果在同一个 `strict-webview` iframe 里渲染，表头文字就在 DOM 里。在 strict-webview 执行这个探测脚本：

```js
(() => {
	const out = [];
	document.querySelectorAll('th').forEach(el => {
		const t = el.innerText.trim();
		if (t) out.push(t);
	});
	document.querySelectorAll('[class*=thead] [class*=cell], [class*=header] [class*=cell], [class*=Head] [class*=cell]').forEach(el => {
		const t = el.innerText.trim();
		if (t && !out.includes(t)) out.push(t);
	});
	console.log(out.length ? "HEADERS: " + out.join(" | ") : "NO_TH_FOUND");
	if (!out.length) {
		// 兜底：搜包含关键字的文本行
		const all = document.body.innerText.split("\n").map(s => s.trim()).filter(s => s && (s.includes("咨询") || s.includes("响应") || s.includes("采纳") || s.includes("日期") || s.includes("流转") || s.includes("卡片") || s.includes("风险")));
		console.log("TEXTS: " + [...new Set(all)].join(" | "));
	}
})()
```

输出里如果是 `HEADERS: 日期 | 咨询人数 | 咨询消息数 | ...`，把整行发我，我按这个顺序和文字做字段映射。如果显示 `NO_TH_FOUND`，说明表格不是 th 渲染（可能是 canvas 或别的结构），再换方式。

### [AI · 2026-08-14 11:59:53]

明白，要多天。接口本来就是按区间返回的，把 `DAYS_BACK` 改成 7（近 7 天，和你页面抓包时一致），同时把中文表头映射整合进脚本，一次跑完直接出中文：

```js
(async () => {
	// ========== 参数配置（只改这里）==========
	const DAYS_BACK = 7;     // 往前追几天：7=近7天，要更多改大（如30=近30天）
	// ========== 工具函数 ==========
	const p2 = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
	const fmtDate = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(d.getDate());
	const now = new Date();
	const start = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - DAYS_BACK);
	const end = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - 1);
	const startTime = fmtDate(start);
	const endTime = fmtDate(end);
	const login = await new Promise(res => {
		try {
			my.getStorage({key:"loginInfo", success:r=>res(r.data||r), fail:()=>res(null)})
		} catch(e) { res(null) }
	});
	const LI = login || {};
	const token = LI.token || "m4_tAjNR3Sw7XpU5t_4DL5qKCNiP9stCRc__";
	const groupId = LI.groupId || "2575699441501575150";
	const thirdShopId = LI.thirdShopId || "2661337551157893458";
	const HEADERS = {client:"qianniu-agent","Content-Type":"application/json",Cookie:"tanyu-agent-account="+token+";tanyu-group-id="+groupId};
	const r = await new Promise(res => {
		my.sendMtop({
			api:"mtop.miniapp.cloud.application.request",
			v:"1.0",
			data:{
				path:"/api/data-service/compass/metrics/query",
				headers:JSON.stringify(HEADERS),
				body:JSON.stringify({thirdShopId,startTime,endTime}),
				method:"POST",
				options:JSON.stringify({timeout:10000,cloudAppId:"56278",env:"online"}),
				sdkVersion:"1.5.9",
				protocols:JSON.stringify({"Content-Type":"application/json","mc-timestamp":String(Date.now()),"mc-env":"online"})
			},
			method:"POST",
			needLogin:true,
			sessionOption:"AutoLoginAndManualLogin",
			success:r=>res({ok:true,raw:r}),
			fail:e=>res({ok:false,raw:e})
		})
	});
	if (!r.ok) {
		console.error("请求失败", r.raw);
		return;
	}
	let service = [], ai = [];
	try {
		const dd = r.raw.data && r.raw.data.data;
		const d = dd && dd.data;
		if (d && d.service && d.service.rows) service = d.service.rows;
		if (d && d.ai && d.ai.rows) ai = d.ai.rows;
	} catch(e) {}
	// ========== 中文表头映射（与页面一致）==========
	const serviceZh = service.map(x => ({
		"日期": x.date,
		"咨询人数": x.service_consult_cnt,
		"咨询消息数": x.service_consult_msg_cnt,
		"三分钟响应率": x.service_3m_response_rate,
		"平均响应时长": x.service_response_avg_sec,
		"平均首次响应时长": x.service_first_response_avg_sec
	}));
	const aiZh = ai.map(x => ({
		"日期": x.date,
		"咨询消息数": x.ai_consult_msg_cnt,
		"Agentic咨询消息数": x.ai_agent_card_event_cnt,
		"话术采纳数": x.ai_consult_response_accept_cnt,
		"话术生成数": x.ai_consult_response_cnt,
		"话术采纳数(自动发送)": x.ai_consult_response_accept_auto_cnt,
		"话术采纳数(手动发送)": x.ai_consult_response_accept_manual_cnt,
		"话术风控拦截数": x.ai_consult_response_risk_cnt,
		"平均自动发送时间": x.ai_consult_response_flow_avg_sec,
		"风控率": x.ai_consult_response_risk_rate,
		"生成率": x.ai_consult_response_rate,
		"采纳率": x.ai_consult_response_accept_rate
	}));
	window.__compass = { service: serviceZh, ai: aiZh, day: startTime + "~" + endTime };
	console.log("RESULT: " + startTime + "~" + endTime + " 服务效率" + serviceZh.length + "天 AI性能" + aiZh.length + "天 数据就绪")
})()
```

执行完看到 RESULT（应是 `服务效率7天 AI性能7天`），再执行：

```js
copy(JSON.stringify(window.__compass))
```

输出就是近 7 天、中文表头的完整数据。要更长时间把 `DAYS_BACK` 改成 30 等即可。

```js
(async () => {
	// ========== 参数配置（只改这里）==========
	const DAYS_BACK = 7;     // 往前追几天：1=昨天, 7=近7天
	// ========== 工具函数 ==========
	const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
	const p2 = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
	const fmt = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(d.getDate()) + " " + p2(d.getHours()) + ":" + p2(d.getMinutes()) + ":" + p2(d.getSeconds());
	const fmtDate = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(d.getDate());
	const now = new Date();
	const start = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - DAYS_BACK);
	const end = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - 1);
	const begin = new Date(start.getFullYear(), start.getMonth(), start.getDate(), 0, 0, 0);
	const endDt = new Date(end.getFullYear(), end.getMonth(), end.getDate(), 23, 59, 59);
	const startTime = fmtDate(start);
	const endTime = fmtDate(end);
	const login = await new Promise(res => {
		try { my.getStorage({key:"loginInfo", success:r=>res(r.data||r), fail:()=>res(null)}) } catch(e) { res(null) }
	});
	const LI = login || {};
	const token = LI.token || "m4_tAjNR3Sw7XpU5t_4DL5qKCNiP9stCRc__";
	const groupId = LI.groupId || "2575699441501575150";
	const thirdShopId = LI.thirdShopId || "2661337551157893458";
	const HEADERS = {client:"qianniu-agent","Content-Type":"application/json",Cookie:"tanyu-agent-account="+token+";tanyu-group-id="+groupId};
	function call(path, body) {
		return new Promise(res => {
			my.sendMtop({
				api:"mtop.miniapp.cloud.application.request",
				v:"1.0",
				data:{
					path:path,
					headers:JSON.stringify(HEADERS),
					body:JSON.stringify(body),
					method:"POST",
					options:JSON.stringify({timeout:10000,cloudAppId:"56278",env:"online"}),
					sdkVersion:"1.5.9",
					protocols:JSON.stringify({"Content-Type":"application/json","mc-timestamp":String(Date.now()),"mc-env":"online"})
				},
				method:"POST",
				needLogin:true,
				sessionOption:"AutoLoginAndManualLogin",
				success:r=>res({ok:true,raw:r}),
				fail:e=>res({ok:false,raw:e})
			})
		})
	}
	function getResults(raw) {
		try {
			const d = raw.data && raw.data.data;
			if (d && d.data && Array.isArray(d.data.results)) return {rows: d.data.results, total: parseInt(d.data.total)||0};
		} catch(e) {}
		return {rows: [], total: 0};
	}
	// ========== agent-trace 拉数 ==========
	async function fetchAll(name, extra) {
		console.log("==== 开始拉取：" + name + " ====");
		const all = [];
		let total = -1, page = 1, empty = 0;
		while (page <= 500) {
			const body = {thirdShopId, pageIndex: page, pageSize: 100, beginTime: fmt(begin), endTime: fmt(endDt), ...extra};
			const r = await call("/api/context/agent-trace/paginate", body);
			if (!r.ok) { console.error("["+name+"] 请求失败", r.raw); break; }
			const {rows, total: t} = getResults(r.raw);
			if (total < 0 && t > 0) total = t;
			if (rows.length) { all.push(...rows); empty = 0; }
			else { empty++; if (empty >= 3) break; }
			console.log("["+name+"] 第"+page+"页 本页"+rows.length+" 累计"+all.length+" 共"+total);
			if (total > 0 && all.length >= total) break;
			if (rows.length < 100 && page > 1) break;
			page++;
			await sleep(300);
		}
		console.log("==== 完成：" + name + " " + all.length + "条 ====");
		return all;
	}
	const withdraw = await fetchAll("已撤回", {ifWithdraw: 1});
	const sent = await fetchAll("侧边栏+编辑后", {sendType: [2,3]});
	const noSend = await fetchAll("未自动发送", {noSendReasonList: [113]});
	// ========== 数据罗盘 ==========
	console.log("==== 开始拉取：数据罗盘 ====");
	const cr = await call("/api/data-service/compass/metrics/query", {thirdShopId, startTime, endTime});
	let service = [], ai = [];
	if (cr.ok) {
		try {
			const dd = cr.raw.data && cr.raw.data.data;
			const d = dd && dd.data;
			if (d && d.service && d.service.rows) service = d.service.rows;
			if (d && d.ai && d.ai.rows) ai = d.ai.rows;
		} catch(e) {}
	} else {
		console.error("数据罗盘请求失败", cr.raw);
	}
	console.log("==== 完成：数据罗盘 服务"+service.length+"天 AI"+ai.length+"天 ====");
	// ========== 组装输出 ==========
	const youhua = [withdraw, sent, noSend].map(a => a.map(x => x.sellerAccount));
	const serviceZh = service.map(x => ({
		"日期": x.date, "咨询人数": x.service_consult_cnt, "咨询消息数": x.service_consult_msg_cnt,
		"三分钟响应率": x.service_3m_response_rate, "平均响应时长": x.service_response_avg_sec, "平均首次响应时长": x.service_first_response_avg_sec
	}));
	const aiZh = ai.map(x => ({
		"日期": x.date, "咨询消息数": x.ai_consult_msg_cnt, "Agentic咨询消息数": x.ai_agent_card_event_cnt,
		"话术采纳数": x.ai_consult_response_accept_cnt, "话术生成数": x.ai_consult_response_cnt,
		"话术采纳数(自动发送)": x.ai_consult_response_accept_auto_cnt, "话术采纳数(手动发送)": x.ai_consult_response_accept_manual_cnt,
		"话术风控拦截数": x.ai_consult_response_risk_cnt, "平均自动发送时间": x.ai_consult_response_flow_avg_sec,
		"风控率": x.ai_consult_response_risk_rate, "生成率": x.ai_consult_response_rate, "采纳率": x.ai_consult_response_accept_rate
	}));
	const s2 = [["日期","咨询人数","咨询消息数","三分钟响应率","平均响应时长","平均首次响应时长"],
		...serviceZh.map(x => [x["日期"],x["咨询人数"],x["咨询消息数"],x["三分钟响应率"],x["平均响应时长"],x["平均首次响应时长"]])];
	const a2 = [["日期","咨询消息数","Agentic咨询消息数","话术采纳数","话术生成数","话术采纳数(自动发送)","话术采纳数(手动发送)","话术风控拦截数","平均自动发送时间","风控率","生成率","采纳率"],
		...aiZh.map(x => [x["日期"],x["咨询消息数"],x["Agentic咨询消息数"],x["话术采纳数"],x["话术生成数"],x["话术采纳数(自动发送)"],x["话术采纳数(手动发送)"],x["话术风控拦截数"],x["平均自动发送时间"],x["风控率"],x["生成率"],x["采纳率"]])];
	window.__daily = {
		"优化": youhua,
		"服务": s2,
		"AI": a2,
		day: startTime + "~" + endTime
	};
	console.log("RESULT: 优化"+withdraw.length+"/"+sent.length+"/"+noSend.length+" 服务"+service.length+"天 AI"+ai.length+"天 数据就绪")
})()
```

### [AI · 2026-08-14 12:07:04]

拆成两个独立配置：`DAYS_BACK` 只控制 `优化`（agent-trace），`服务`/`AI`（数据罗盘）新增 `COMPASS_START` 起始日期，从这天一直拉到昨天，按月分段请求防止接口单次区间上限：

```js
(async () => {
	// ========== 参数配置（只改这里）==========
	const DAYS_BACK = 7;          // 优化：往前追几天（1=昨天）
	const COMPASS_START = "2024-01-01";  // 服务/AI：从这天拉到昨天（要全部历史就保持）
	// ========== 工具函数 ==========
	const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
	const p2 = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
	const fmt = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(d.getDate()) + " " + p2(d.getHours()) + ":" + p2(d.getMinutes()) + ":" + p2(d.getSeconds());
	const fmtDate = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(d.getDate());
	const now = new Date();
	const start = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - DAYS_BACK);
	const end = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - 1);
	const begin = new Date(start.getFullYear(), start.getMonth(), start.getDate(), 0, 0, 0);
	const endDt = new Date(end.getFullYear(), end.getMonth(), end.getDate(), 23, 59, 59);
	const startTime = fmtDate(start);
	const endTime = fmtDate(end);
	const login = await new Promise(res => {
		try { my.getStorage({key:"loginInfo", success:r=>res(r.data||r), fail:()=>res(null)}) } catch(e) { res(null) }
	});
	const LI = login || {};
	const token = LI.token || "m4_tAjNR3Sw7XpU5t_4DL5qKCNiP9stCRc__";
	const groupId = LI.groupId || "2575699441501575150";
	const thirdShopId = LI.thirdShopId || "2661337551157893458";
	const HEADERS = {client:"qianniu-agent","Content-Type":"application/json",Cookie:"tanyu-agent-account="+token+";tanyu-group-id="+groupId};
	function call(path, body) {
		return new Promise(res => {
			my.sendMtop({
				api:"mtop.miniapp.cloud.application.request",
				v:"1.0",
				data:{
					path:path,
					headers:JSON.stringify(HEADERS),
					body:JSON.stringify(body),
					method:"POST",
					options:JSON.stringify({timeout:10000,cloudAppId:"56278",env:"online"}),
					sdkVersion:"1.5.9",
					protocols:JSON.stringify({"Content-Type":"application/json","mc-timestamp":String(Date.now()),"mc-env":"online"})
				},
				method:"POST",
				needLogin:true,
				sessionOption:"AutoLoginAndManualLogin",
				success:r=>res({ok:true,raw:r}),
				fail:e=>res({ok:false,raw:e})
			})
		})
	}
	function getResults(raw) {
		try {
			const d = raw.data && raw.data.data;
			if (d && d.data && Array.isArray(d.data.results)) return {rows: d.data.results, total: parseInt(d.data.total)||0};
		} catch(e) {}
		return {rows: [], total: 0};
	}
	// ========== agent-trace 拉数（受 DAYS_BACK 限制）==========
	async function fetchAll(name, extra) {
		console.log("==== 开始拉取：" + name + " ====");
		const all = [];
		let total = -1, page = 1, empty = 0;
		while (page <= 500) {
			const body = {thirdShopId, pageIndex: page, pageSize: 100, beginTime: fmt(begin), endTime: fmt(endDt), ...extra};
			const r = await call("/api/context/agent-trace/paginate", body);
			if (!r.ok) { console.error("["+name+"] 请求失败", r.raw); break; }
			const {rows, total: t} = getResults(r.raw);
			if (total < 0 && t > 0) total = t;
			if (rows.length) { all.push(...rows); empty = 0; }
			else { empty++; if (empty >= 3) break; }
			console.log("["+name+"] 第"+page+"页 本页"+rows.length+" 累计"+all.length+" 共"+total);
			if (total > 0 && all.length >= total) break;
			if (rows.length < 100 && page > 1) break;
			page++;
			await sleep(300);
		}
		console.log("==== 完成：" + name + " " + all.length + "条 ====");
		return all;
	}
	// ========== 数据罗盘（不受 DAYS_BACK 限制，按月分段拉全量）==========
	async function fetchCompass() {
		console.log("==== 开始拉取：数据罗盘 ====");
		const allS = [], allA = [];
		let cur = new Date(COMPASS_START);
		let seg = 0;
		while (cur <= end) {
			seg++;
			const y = cur.getFullYear(), m = cur.getMonth();
			const lastDay = new Date(y, m + 1, 0);
			const segEnd = lastDay > end ? end : lastDay;
			const cr = await call("/api/data-service/compass/metrics/query", {thirdShopId, startTime: fmtDate(cur), endTime: fmtDate(segEnd)});
			if (cr.ok) {
				try {
					const dd = cr.raw.data && cr.raw.data.data;
					const d = dd && dd.data;
					if (d && d.service && d.service.rows) allS.push(...d.service.rows);
					if (d && d.ai && d.ai.rows) allA.push(...d.ai.rows);
				} catch(e) {}
			} else {
				console.error("数据罗盘分段请求失败", fmtDate(cur), "~", fmtDate(segEnd), cr.raw);
			}
			console.log("[罗盘] 第"+seg+"段 " + fmtDate(cur) + "~" + fmtDate(segEnd) + " 服务累计"+allS.length+" AI累计"+allA.length);
			cur = new Date(y, m + 1, 1);
			await sleep(300);
		}
		console.log("==== 完成：数据罗盘 服务"+allS.length+"天 AI"+allA.length+"天 ====");
		return {service: allS, ai: allA};
	}
	const withdraw = await fetchAll("已撤回", {ifWithdraw: 1});
	const sent = await fetchAll("侧边栏+编辑后", {sendType: [2,3]});
	const noSend = await fetchAll("未自动发送", {noSendReasonList: [113]});
	const compass = await fetchCompass();
	// ========== 组装输出 ==========
	const youhua = [withdraw, sent, noSend].map(a => a.map(x => x.sellerAccount));
	const serviceZh = compass.service.map(x => ({
		"日期": x.date, "咨询人数": x.service_consult_cnt, "咨询消息数": x.service_consult_msg_cnt,
		"三分钟响应率": x.service_3m_response_rate, "平均响应时长": x.service_response_avg_sec, "平均首次响应时长": x.service_first_response_avg_sec
	}));
	const aiZh = compass.ai.map(x => ({
		"日期": x.date, "咨询消息数": x.ai_consult_msg_cnt, "Agentic咨询消息数": x.ai_agent_card_event_cnt,
		"话术采纳数": x.ai_consult_response_accept_cnt, "话术生成数": x.ai_consult_response_cnt,
		"话术采纳数(自动发送)": x.ai_consult_response_accept_auto_cnt, "话术采纳数(手动发送)": x.ai_consult_response_accept_manual_cnt,
		"话术风控拦截数": x.ai_consult_response_risk_cnt, "平均自动发送时间": x.ai_consult_response_flow_avg_sec,
		"风控率": x.ai_consult_response_risk_rate, "生成率": x.ai_consult_response_rate, "采纳率": x.ai_consult_response_accept_rate
	}));
	const s2 = [["日期","咨询人数","咨询消息数","三分钟响应率","平均响应时长","平均首次响应时长"],
		...serviceZh.map(x => [x["日期"],x["咨询人数"],x["咨询消息数"],x["三分钟响应率"],x["平均响应时长"],x["平均首次响应时长"]])];
	const a2 = [["日期","咨询消息数","Agentic咨询消息数","话术采纳数","话术生成数","话术采纳数(自动发送)","话术采纳数(手动发送)","话术风控拦截数","平均自动发送时间","风控率","生成率","采纳率"],
		...aiZh.map(x => [x["日期"],x["咨询消息数"],x["Agentic咨询消息数"],x["话术采纳数"],x["话术生成数"],x["话术采纳数(自动发送)"],x["话术采纳数(手动发送)"],x["话术风控拦截数"],x["平均自动发送时间"],x["风控率"],x["生成率"],x["采纳率"]])];
	window.__daily = {
		"优化": youhua,
		"服务": s2,
		"AI": a2,
		day: startTime + "~" + endTime
	};
	console.log("RESULT: 优化"+withdraw.length+"/"+sent.length+"/"+noSend.length+" 服务"+compass.service.length+"天 AI"+compass.ai.length+"天 数据就绪")
})()
```

改动说明：
- `DAYS_BACK` 只影响 `优化`（agent-trace 三个筛选），默认 7 天，改 1 就是昨天单天
- `COMPASS_START` 控制 `服务`/`AI` 起始日期，默认 `2024-01-01`，一直拉到昨天，按月分段自动合并，不受 DAYS_BACK 影响
- 输出结构不变：`{优化, 服务, AI, day}`

### [用户 · 2026-08-14 12:09:08]

怎么停止数据获取

### [AI · 2026-08-14 12:09:14]

DevTools 控制台里异步脚本没有"停止"按钮，最直接的办法：

1. **刷新页面**（F5 或 Ctrl+R）——立即终止所有正在执行的脚本和请求，`window.__daily` 等变量会清空
2. 或者关掉 DevTools 面板（脚本可能仍在后台跑，要彻底停还是刷新页面）

刷新后重新执行脚本即可。如果要防止下次拉太多，先把 `COMPASS_START` 改成实际需要的起始日期（比如 `"2026-08-01"`），`DAYS_BACK` 按需调小。

```js
			if (!r.ok) { console.error("["+name+"] 请求失败", r.raw); break; }
			const {rows, total: t} = getResults(r.raw);
			if (total < 0 && t > 0) total = t;
			if (rows.length) { all.push(...rows); empty = 0; }
			else { empty++; if (empty >= 3) break; }
			console.log("["+name+"] 第"+page+"页 本页"+rows.length+" 累计"+all.length+" 共"+total);
			if (total > 0 && all.length >= total) break;
			if (rows.length < 100 && page > 1) break;
			page++;
			await sleep(300);
		}
		console.log("==== 完成：" + name + " " + all.length + "条 ====");
		return all;
	}
	// ========== 数据罗盘 ==========
	async function fetchCompass() {
		console.log("==== 开始拉取：数据罗盘 ====");
		const cr = await call("/api/data-service/compass/metrics/query", {thirdShopId, startTime: dayStr, endTime: dayStr});
		let service = [], ai = [];
		if (cr.ok) {
			try {
				const dd = cr.raw.data && cr.raw.data.data;
				const d = dd && dd.data;
				if (d && d.service && d.service.rows) service = d.service.rows;
				if (d && d.ai && d.ai.rows) ai = d.ai.rows;
			} catch(e) {}
		} else {
			console.error("数据罗盘请求失败", cr.raw);
		}
		console.log("==== 完成：数据罗盘 服务"+service.length+"天 AI"+ai.length+"天 ====");
		return {service, ai};
	}
	const withdraw = await fetchAll("已撤回", {ifWithdraw: 1});
	const sent = await fetchAll("侧边栏+编辑后", {sendType: [2,3]});
	const noSend = await fetchAll("未自动发送", {noSendReasonList: [113]});
	const compass = await fetchCompass();
	// ========== 组装输出 ==========
	const youhua = [withdraw, sent, noSend].map(a => a.map(x => x.sellerAccount));
	const serviceZh = compass.service.map(x => ({
		"日期": x.date, "咨询人数": x.service_consult_cnt, "咨询消息数": x.service_consult_msg_cnt,
		"三分钟响应率": x.service_3m_response_rate, "平均响应时长": x.service_response_avg_sec, "平均首次响应时长": x.service_first_response_avg_sec
	}));
	const aiZh = compass.ai.map(x => ({
		"日期": x.date, "咨询消息数": x.ai_consult_msg_cnt, "Agentic咨询消息数": x.ai_agent_card_event_cnt,
		"话术采纳数": x.ai_consult_response_accept_cnt, "话术生成数": x.ai_consult_response_cnt,
		"话术采纳数(自动发送)": x.ai_consult_response_accept_auto_cnt, "话术采纳数(手动发送)": x.ai_consult_response_accept_manual_cnt,
		"话术风控拦截数": x.ai_consult_response_risk_cnt, "平均自动发送时间": x.ai_consult_response_flow_avg_sec,
		"风控率": x.ai_consult_response_risk_rate, "生成率": x.ai_consult_response_rate, "采纳率": x.ai_consult_response_accept_rate
	}));
	const s2 = [["日期","咨询人数","咨询消息数","三分钟响应率","平均响应时长","平均首次响应时长"],
		...serviceZh.map(x => [x["日期"],x["咨询人数"],x["咨询消息数"],x["三分钟响应率"],x["平均响应时长"],x["平均首次响应时长"]])];
	const a2 = [["日期","咨询消息数","Agentic咨询消息数","话术采纳数","话术生成数","话术采纳数(自动发送)","话术采纳数(手动发送)","话术风控拦截数","平均自动发送时间","风控率","生成率","采纳率"],
		...aiZh.map(x => [x["日期"],x["咨询消息数"],x["Agentic咨询消息数"],x["话术采纳数"],x["话术生成数"],x["话术采纳数(自动发送)"],x["话术采纳数(手动发送)"],x["话术风控拦截数"],x["平均自动发送时间"],x["风控率"],x["生成率"],x["采纳率"]])];
	window.__daily = {
		"优化": youhua,
		"服务": s2,
		"AI": a2,
		day: dayStr
	};
	console.log("RESULT: 优化"+withdraw.length+"/"+sent.length+"/"+noSend.length+" 服务"+compass.service.length+"天 AI"+compass.ai.length+"天 数据就绪")
})()
```

说明：
- `DAYS_BACK` 唯一时间配置：1=昨天一整天，2=前天一整天，`优化` 和 `服务`/`AI` 取同一天
- 去掉了 COMPASS_START 和按月分段，数据罗盘只请求目标日单天
- 输出结构不变：`{优化, 服务, AI, day}`

```js
	// ========== 参数配置（只改这里）==========

	// ========== 工具函数 ==========

	const p2 = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
	const fmt = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(d.getDate()) + " " + p2(d.getHours()) + ":" + p2(d.getMinutes()) + ":" + p2(d.getSeconds());
	const fmtDate = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(d.getDate());
	const now = new Date();
	const target = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - DAYS_BACK);
	const begin = new Date(target.getFullYear(), target.getMonth(), target.getDate(), 0, 0, 0);
	const endDt = new Date(target.getFullYear(), target.getMonth(), target.getDate(), 23, 59, 59);
	const dayStr = fmtDate(target);
	const login = await new Promise(res => {
		try { my.getStorage({key:"loginInfo", success:r=>res(r.data||r), fail:()=>res(null)}) } catch(e) { res(null) }
	});
	const LI = login || {};
	const token = LI.token || "m4_tAjNR3Sw7XpU5t_4DL5qKCNiP9stCRc__";
	const groupId = LI.groupId || "2575699441501575150";
	const thirdShopId = LI.thirdShopId || "2661337551157893458";
	const HEADERS = {client:"qianniu-agent","Content-Type":"application/json",Cookie:"tanyu-agent-account="+token+";tanyu-group-id="+groupId};
	function call(path, body) {

			my.sendMtop({
				api:"mtop.miniapp.cloud.application.request",
				v:"1.0",
				data:{
					path:path,
					headers:JSON.stringify(HEADERS),
					body:JSON.stringify(body),
					method:"POST",
					options:JSON.stringify({timeout:10000,cloudAppId:"56278",env:"online"}),
					sdkVersion:"1.5.9",
					protocols:JSON.stringify({"Content-Type":"application/json","mc-timestamp":String(Date.now()),"mc-env":"online"})
				},
				method:"POST",
				needLogin:true,
				sessionOption:"AutoLoginAndManualLogin",
				success:r=>res({ok:true,raw:r}),
				fail:e=>res({ok:false,raw:e})
			})
		})
	}
	function getResults(raw) {
		try {
			const d = raw.data && raw.data.data;
			if (d && d.data && Array.isArray(d.data.results)) return {rows: d.data.results, total: parseInt(d.data.total)||0};
		} catch(e) {}
		return {rows: [], total: 0};
	}
	// ========== agent-trace 拉数 ==========
	async function fetchAll(name, extra) {
		console.log("==== 开始拉取：" + name + " ====");

			const r = await call("/api/context/agent-trace/paginate", body);
			if (!r.ok) { console.error("["+name+"] 请求失败", r.raw); break; }
			const {rows, total: t} = getResults(r.raw);
			if (total < 0 && t > 0) total = t;
			if (rows.length) { all.push(...rows); empty = 0; }
			else { empty++; if (empty >= 3) break; }
			console.log("["+name+"] 第"+page+"页 本页"+rows.length+" 累计"+all.length+" 共"+total);
			if (total > 0 && all.length >= total) break;
			if (rows.length < 100 && page > 1) break;
			page++;
			await sleep(300);
		}
		console.log("==== 完成：" + name + " " + all.length + "条 ====");
		return all;
	}
	// ========== 数据罗盘 ==========
	async function fetchCompass() {
		console.log("==== 开始拉取：数据罗盘 ====");
		const cr = await call("/api/data-service/compass/metrics/query", {thirdShopId, startTime: dayStr, endTime: dayStr});
		let service = [], ai = [];
		if (cr.ok) {
			try {
				const dd = cr.raw.data && cr.raw.data.data;
				const d = dd && dd.data;
				if (d && d.service && d.service.rows) service = d.service.rows;
				if (d && d.ai && d.ai.rows) ai = d.ai.rows;
			} catch(e) {}
		} else {
			console.error("数据罗盘请求失败", cr.raw);
		}
		console.log("==== 完成：数据罗盘 服务"+service.length+"天 AI"+ai.length+"天 ====");
		return {service, ai};
	}
	const withdraw = await fetchAll("已撤回", {ifWithdraw: 1});
	const sent = await fetchAll("侧边栏+编辑后", {sendType: [2,3]});
	const noSend = await fetchAll("未自动发送", {noSendReasonList: [113]});
	const compass = await fetchCompass();
	// ========== 组装输出（服务/AI 一维列表，纯值）==========
	const youhua = [withdraw, sent, noSend].map(a => a.map(x => x.sellerAccount));
	const sx = compass.service[0] || {};
	const ax = compass.ai[0] || {};
	const s1 = [sx.date, sx.service_consult_cnt, sx.service_consult_msg_cnt, sx.service_3m_response_rate, sx.service_response_avg_sec, sx.service_first_response_avg_sec];
	const a1 = [ax.date, ax.ai_consult_msg_cnt, ax.ai_agent_card_event_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_accept_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_accept_auto_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_accept_manual_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_risk_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_flow_avg_sec, ax.ai_consult_response_risk_rate, ax.ai_consult_response_rate, ax.ai_consult_response_accept_rate];
	window.__daily = {

		day: dayStr
	};
	console.log("RESULT: 优化"+withdraw.length+"/"+sent.length+"/"+noSend.length+" 服务"+(sx.date?1:0)+"天 AI"+(ax.date?1:0)+"天 数据就绪")
})()
```

```js
	const endDt = new Date(target.getFullYear(), target.getMonth(), target.getDate(), 23, 59, 59);
	const dayStr = fmtDate(target);
	const login = await new Promise(res => {
		try { my.getStorage({key:"loginInfo", success:r=>res(r.data||r), fail:()=>res(null)}) } catch(e) { res(null) }
	});
	const LI = login || {};
	const token = LI.token || "m4_tAjNR3Sw7XpU5t_4DL5qKCNiP9stCRc__";
	const groupId = LI.groupId || "2575699441501575150";
	const thirdShopId = LI.thirdShopId || "2661337551157893458";
	const HEADERS = {client:"qianniu-agent","Content-Type":"application/json",Cookie:"tanyu-agent-account="+token+";tanyu-group-id="+groupId};
	function call(path, body) {
		return new Promise(res => {
			my.sendMtop({
				api:"mtop.miniapp.cloud.application.request",
				v:"1.0",
				data:{
					path:path,
					headers:JSON.stringify(HEADERS),
					body:JSON.stringify(body),
					method:"POST",
					options:JSON.stringify({timeout:10000,cloudAppId:"56278",env:"online"}),
					sdkVersion:"1.5.9",
					protocols:JSON.stringify({"Content-Type":"application/json","mc-timestamp":String(Date.now()),"mc-env":"online"})
				},
				method:"POST",
				needLogin:true,
				sessionOption:"AutoLoginAndManualLogin",
				success:r=>res({ok:true,raw:r}),
				fail:e=>res({ok:false,raw:e})
			})
		})
	}
	function getResults(raw) {
		try {
			const d = raw.data && raw.data.data;
			if (d && d.data && Array.isArray(d.data.results)) return {rows: d.data.results, total: parseInt(d.data.total)||0};
		} catch(e) {}
		return {rows: [], total: 0};
	}
	// ========== agent-trace 拉数 ==========
	async function fetchAll(name, extra) {
		console.log("==== 开始拉取：" + name + " ====");
		const all = [];
		let total = -1, page = 1, empty = 0;
		while (page <= 500) {
			const body = {thirdShopId, pageIndex: page, pageSize: 100, beginTime: fmt(begin), endTime: fmt(endDt), ...extra};
			const r = await call("/api/context/agent-trace/paginate", body);
			if (!r.ok) { console.error("["+name+"] 请求失败", r.raw); break; }
			const {rows, total: t} = getResults(r.raw);
			if (total < 0 && t > 0) total = t;
			if (rows.length) { all.push(...rows); empty = 0; }
			else { empty++; if (empty >= 3) break; }
			console.log("["+name+"] 第"+page+"页 本页"+rows.length+" 累计"+all.length+" 共"+total);
			if (total > 0 && all.length >= total) break;
			if (rows.length < 100 && page > 1) break;
			page++;
			await sleep(300);
		}
		console.log("==== 完成：" + name + " " + all.length + "条 ====");
		return all;
	}
	// ========== 数据罗盘 ==========
	async function fetchCompass() {
		console.log("==== 开始拉取：数据罗盘 ====");
		const cr = await call("/api/data-service/compass/metrics/query", {thirdShopId, startTime: dayStr, endTime: dayStr});
		let service = [], ai = [];
		if (cr.ok) {
			try {
				const dd = cr.raw.data && cr.raw.data.data;
				const d = dd && dd.data;
				if (d && d.service && d.service.rows) service = d.service.rows;
				if (d && d.ai && d.ai.rows) ai = d.ai.rows;
			} catch(e) {}
		} else {
			console.error("数据罗盘请求失败", cr.raw);
		}
		console.log("==== 完成：数据罗盘 服务"+service.length+"天 AI"+ai.length+"天 ====");
		return {service, ai};
	}
	const withdraw = await fetchAll("已撤回", {ifWithdraw: 1});
	const sent = await fetchAll("侧边栏+编辑后", {sendType: [2,3]});
	const noSend = await fetchAll("未自动发送", {noSendReasonList: [113]});
	const compass = await fetchCompass();
	// ========== 组装输出（服务/AI：先全部表头、再全部表值）==========
	const youhua = [withdraw, sent, noSend].map(a => a.map(x => x.sellerAccount));
	const SERVICE_HEADERS = ["日期","咨询人数","咨询消息数","三分钟响应率","平均响应时长","平均首次响应时长"];
	const AI_HEADERS = ["日期","咨询消息数","Agentic咨询消息数","话术采纳数","话术生成数","话术采纳数(自动发送)","话术采纳数(手动发送)","话术风控拦截数","平均自动发送时间","风控率","生成率","采纳率"];
	const sx = compass.service[0] || {};
	const ax = compass.ai[0] || {};
	const sv = [sx.date, sx.service_consult_cnt, sx.service_consult_msg_cnt, sx.service_3m_response_rate, sx.service_response_avg_sec, sx.service_first_response_avg_sec];
	const av = [ax.date, ax.ai_consult_msg_cnt, ax.ai_agent_card_event_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_accept_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_accept_auto_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_accept_manual_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_risk_cnt, ax.ai_consult_response_flow_avg_sec, ax.ai_consult_response_risk_rate, ax.ai_consult_response_rate, ax.ai_consult_response_accept_rate];
	const s1 = [...SERVICE_HEADERS, ...sv];
	const a1 = [...AI_HEADERS, ...av];
	window.__daily = {
		"优化": youhua,
		"服务": s1,
		"AI": a1,
		day: dayStr
	};
	console.log("RESULT: 优化"+withdraw.length+"/"+sent.length+"/"+noSend.length+" 服务"+(sx.date?1:0)+"天 AI"+(ax.date?1:0)+"天 数据就绪")
})()
```

### [用户 · 2026-08-14 17:38:15]

你这样算JS逆向吗

### [AI · 2026-08-14 17:38:23]

算，但属于比较轻量的一类。具体定位：

**算逆向的部分**
- 拦截并读取了 `my.sendMtop` 的入参，还原了 `agent-trace/paginate`、`compass/metrics/query` 两个接口的请求结构（path / headers / body / 鉴权头）
- 反向推导出响应数据的嵌套结构（`raw.data.data.data`、三层 data、字段枚举）
- 这是标准的"接口逆向"流程：抓包 -> 定位调用点 -> 还原参数 -> 构造请求

**不算典型 JS 逆向的部分**
- 没做代码混淆还原（没有反混淆、AST 还原）
- 没破解加密算法或 sign 签名（这些接口没有强签名）
- 没有独立重写请求逻辑，而是**直接复用应用自身的 `my.sendMtop` 通道**在页面上下文里发起请求

所以准确说法是：**基于 DevTools 的 hook 注入 + 接口复用型逆向**。它比纯抓包深一层（动了运行时函数），但比"还原加密算法、脱离客户端重放"那种浅得多——也因此更稳，因为走的还是应用自己的鉴权和通道，不需要伪造签名。

一句话：算接口逆向的入门形态，靠运行环境现成通道拿数据，不是硬核的算法破解。

### [用户 · 2026-08-14 17:44:38]

这个miniapp是否可以直接通过网页访问做到呢，就算网页这个加载不全，js脚本获取的信息是否能够伪造环境登录呢

### [AI · 2026-08-14 17:44:55]

分两个问题答：

**1. 能否直接网页访问**

基本不行，没有公开网页版路径。理由：
- 探域智能体是千牛客户端内的淘宝小程序，运行在 CEF 容器里，不是常规 H5 站点
- 千牛有网页版工作台，但这类"客户端内嵌小程序"通常不提供浏览器独立入口
- 就算有对应 H5，大概率也是另一个域名/另一套接口，且同样需要商家登录态 + 环境风控

之前实测也验证了这一点：Python 直连 `miniapp-metadata.taobao.com` 被反爬重定向回淘宝首页，说明网关会校验来源环境（UA、referer、cookie 完整性等），不是浏览器直接 POST 就能过的。

**2. JS 伪造环境登录是否可行**

理论上可行，但比当前方案重得多，且有三层门槛：

| 门槛 | 说明 | 难度 |
|------|------|------|
| 登录态 | loginInfo 的 token 是后端签发，无法伪造；但可以**复用已登录设备的 token**（你手上已有，一直有效），这层实际可绕过 | 低 |
| mtop 网关签名 | my.sendMtop 走 mtop 协议，直接 HTTP 重放通常需要 x-sign 等签名参数 + appKey。部分接口签名校验弱或可离线算出（mtop sign 算法是公开的），但探域走的是 `mtop.miniapp.cloud.application.request` 这个云应用通道，签名链更复杂 | 中高 |
| 风控/环境指纹 | 网关会校验 deviceId、容器标识、时间戳一致性等，纯浏览器伪造容易被识别 | 高 |

还有一个思路：在浏览器里**mock 小程序容器**——自己实现一个假的 `window.my`（getStorage / sendMtop），加载小程序编译产物跑起来。但这需要从千牛客户端提取小程序包源码（`.axml/.js` 打包产物，通常加密/混淆），成本远高于现状。

**结论**：当前 DevTools 方案（在应用自身环境里复用 `my.sendMtop`）天然通过签名、风控和登录态，是最优解。脱离客户端重放属于"能做但投入产出比极低"，除非你有明确需求（比如要部署到无千牛的服务器上跑），否则不建议走这条路。

```js
	const fmtDate = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + p2(d.getDate());

	const dayStr = fmtDate(target);
	const login = await new Promise(res => {

	});

	function call(path, body) {
		return new Promise(res => {
			my.sendMtop({
				api:"mtop.miniapp.cloud.application.request",
				v:"1.0",
				data:{
					path:path,
					headers:JSON.stringify(HEADERS),
					body:JSON.stringify(body),
					method:"POST",

					sdkVersion:"1.5.9",
					protocols:JSON.stringify({"Content-Type":"application/json","mc-timestamp":String(Date.now()),"mc-env":"online"})
				},
				method:"POST",
				needLogin:true,
				sessionOption:"AutoLoginAndManualLogin",
				success:r=>res({ok:true,raw:r}),
				fail:e=>res({ok:false,raw:e})
			})
		})
	}
	function getResults(raw) {
		try {
			const d = raw.data && raw.data.data;
			if (d && d.data && Array.isArray(d.data.results)) return {rows: d.data.results, total: parseInt(d.data.total)||0};
		} catch(e) {}

		console.log("==== 开始拉取：" + name + " ====");

			const r = await call("/api/context/agent-trace/paginate", body);
			if (!r.ok) { console.error("["+name+"] 请求失败", r.raw); break; }
			const {rows, total: t} = getResults(r.raw);
			if (total < 0 && t > 0) total = t;
			if (rows.length) { all.push(...rows); empty = 0; }
			else { empty++; if (empty >= 3) break; }
			console.log("["+name+"] 第"+page+"页 本页"+rows.length+" 累计"+all.length+" 共"+total);
			if (total > 0 && all.length >= total) break;

})()
```

### [AI · 2026-08-18 10:33:08]

`my is not defined` 说明执行上下文不在 `strict-webview` iframe 里了（刷新页面或重新打开 DevTools 后，控制台默认回到顶层 top 上下文，而 `my` 只存在于 strict-webview 里）。

修复方法：DevTools Console 面板**左上角的下拉框**（默认显示 `top`），点开选择包含 `strict-webview` 的 iframe 上下文，再重新粘贴执行脚本。

如果下拉框里找不到，可以先用这段确认是否切对（能打印 true 就是对的）：

```js
typeof my !== "undefined"
```

返回 `true` 后再跑脚本。
